word-wrap: break-word;'>FutuBull</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Returns</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cumulative market-adjusted stock returns in the past  $ [-4d, 0] $ window for a given stock on each date</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ [-4d, 0] $</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cumulative market-adjusted stock returns in the past  $ [-25d, -5d] $</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Returns</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cumulative market-adjusted stock returns in the past  $ [-251d, -26d] $</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ [-251d, -26d] $</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cumulative market-adjusted stock returns in the past  $ [-251d, -26d] $ window for a given stock on each date</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ln(IdioVol)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The natural logarithm of idiosyncratic return volatility over the past 30-day rolling window for a given stock on each date</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ln(Turnover)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The natural logarithm of trading volume over the past five-day rolling window for a given stock on each date</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \left|\Delta H_{olding}\right| $</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The absolute value of  $ \Delta H_{holding} $ during the two-day window  $ [0,+1] $ after the release of results announcements</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>CCASS</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Net Buy</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>An indicator that equals one if  $ \Delta H_{holding} $ during the two-day window  $ [0,+1] $ after the release of results announcements is positive</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>CCASS</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Trade Profitability (3-month)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cumulative market-adjusted trading returns in the three-month window  $ [+2, +90] $ assuming that investors hold the traded positions for three months from their significant net trades conducted during the two-day window  $ [0,+1] $ after the release of Accounting Documents</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Trade Profitability (6-month)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cumulative market-adjusted trading returns in the six-month window  $ [+2, +182] $ assuming that investors hold the traded positions for six months from their significant net trades conducted during the two-day window  $ [0,+1] $ after the release of Accounting Documents</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Returns</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cumulative market-adjusted stock returns in the three-month window  $ [+2, +90] $ if the sign is consistent with the cumulative market-adjusted stock returns during the two-day window  $ [0,+1] $ after the release of Results Announcements and the magnitude of the announcement returns is over 5%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Returns</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cumulative market-adjusted stock returns in the three-month window  $ [+2, +182] $ if the sign is consistent with the cumulative market-adjusted stock returns during the two-day window  $ [0,+1] $ after the release of Results Announcements and the magnitude of the announcement returns is over 5%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Futu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>An indicator that equals one if the broker is Futu, zero if otherwise</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>CCASS</td></tr></table>



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Variable</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Description</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Source</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $Stake\% (Broker)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Percentage change in aggregate positions in the direction consistent with trade profitability held by investors at the specified broker (Broker) during the two-day window [0,+1] after the release of results announcements, where Broker can be Futu, I-Access, or bulge bracket investment banks without significant retail businesses in Hong Kong (Bulge Bracket) including Goldman Sachs, Morgan Stanley, JPMorgan Chase, Barclays, Bank of America (ML), Deutsche Bank, UBS, Credit Suisse</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>CCASS</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>#Futu (&gt;x)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The number of times  $ \Delta $Stake\% (Futu) is more than x% under the specified circumstances</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>CCASS, Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>#I-Access (&gt;x)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The number of times  $ \Delta $Stake\% (I-Access) is more than x% under the specified circumstances</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>CCASS, Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>#Bulge Bracket (&gt;x)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The number of times  $ \Delta $Stake\% (Bulge Bracket) is more than x% under the specified circumstances, where Bulge Bracket includes Goldman Sachs, Morgan Stanley, JPMorgan Chase, Barclays, Bank of America (ML), Deutsche Bank, UBS, Credit Suisse</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>CCASS, Capital IQ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>IPE</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Intraperiod efficiency is calculated as an area-under-curve measure of the speed of price adjustment within the [0,+5] window after earnings announcement using the average of: [1 - ( $ |AR_{s} - AR_{i}| $)/|AR_{s}|], where AR is the abnormal return over [0,t].</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Capital IQ</td></tr></table>

##### APPENDIX B: FUTUBULL TRADING PLATFORM INTERFACE

Appendix B provides some snapshots of the integrated trading platform interface at FutuBull from March 12 to March 15, 2022. The main language used for the FutuBull platform is Chinese. The following snapshots display the interface after turning on the autotranslation feature within the mobile application to translate most Chinese content to English.

#### Desktop Application Interface (March 12)

- The “News” tab provides timely news feeds of media articles and the major types of informative regulatory filings related to each company.

• Informative regulatory filings such as Results Announcements, Financial Reports, and Results Guidance are displayed in the news feeds.

• Uninformative regulatory filings such as “monthly return of equity issuer on movement in securities” are not displayed in the news feeds.

##### APPENDIX C: TIMING OF INFORMATION ACCESS

To evaluate the timing of information access by users of the FutuBull platform, I set up a bot to track the views on a small sample of recent articles every 24 hours to examine the trends in daily views over time. From the list of “hot” stocks on FutuBull after the market closes on December 28, January 10, and January 12, I extract the most recent 10 articles on these companies, respectively, and subsequently try to record the number of views for each article over time. The number of views is automatically recorded every 24 hours until mid-March. $ ^{31} $ Figure C-1 displays the mean (colored in orange)

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-15//88ea7abb-f440-4c72-be3f-1913b7d8fe1f/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_chart_box_176_155_828_562.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKzReLNvew3ySINYJ0fuAMN%2F2026-04-04T14%3A58%3A29Z%2F-1%2F%2Fb74365dbc7786b35675a2b323ad37b0737455ce22974a4f1f681e66268f24b16" alt="Image" width="67%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Fig. C-1.—Percentage Increase in Daily Views from Article Release Date</div> </div>


and median (colored in gray) daily percentage increases in the number of views on these articles by the number of days since the article's publication date.

For the medium article, the pattern shows that over 99% of the views occur within the first two days of the article release date over the specified window tracked. On average, roughly 80% of the views occur within the first two days of the article release date over the tracked window. Roughly 88% of the views occur within the first seven days of the article release date over the tracked window.

From another perspective, I also compare the cumulative views on articles for a sample of firms between two snapshots that are roughly 162 days apart to assess the information access patterns by the staleness of content on the FutuBull platform. For a list of 182 randomly chosen sample firms, I obtained two snapshots of the cumulative views on all the articles published seven days before the snapshot dates in August 2021 and January 2022, respectively, from these sample firms. $ ^{32} $ Below I compare the changes in the cumulative views in this span of 162 days in figure C-2 by the staleness of information measured on a quarterly basis.

## 1444 J. CHAU

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-15//88ea7abb-f440-4c72-be3f-1913b7d8fe1f/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_chart_box_137_152_794_364.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKzReLNvew3ySINYJ0fuAMN%2F2026-04-04T14%3A58%3A29Z%2F-1%2F%2F7ae2f62df12c674f551d693a5a99f377fa9c8a63eeee306b9318105f7e5c6528" alt="Image" width="67%" /></div>

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-15//88ea7abb-f440-4c72-be3f-1913b7d8fe1f/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_chart_box_138_447_793_651.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKzReLNvew3ySINYJ0fuAMN%2F2026-04-04T14%3A58%3A29Z%2F-1%2F%2F12b380d1a5c2600d0185df588f2640b6462dfe17700f19ccc1261a16cf6f9bbc" alt="Image" width="67%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">FIG. C-3.—Percentage Increase in the Cumulative Views of Financial Reports</div> </div>


For the same list of 182 randomly chosen sample firms, I also look into the cumulative views on a subsample of their financial reports as of the snapshot dates in August 2021 and January 2022, respectively. Similarly, I compare the changes in cumulative views in this span of 162 days in figure C-3 by the staleness of financial reports measured on a semi-annual basis.

These patterns suggest that users of the FutuBull platform are generally interested in accessing timely information. They have similar interests in accessing stale financial reports relative to an average stale article that is within two-quarters of staleness.

## APPENDIX D: GROWTH OF INTEGRATED TRADING PLATFORM FOR RETAIL INVESTORS

Self-directed trading platforms have experienced rapid growth in many regions, without exception to the equity market in HK. Figure D-1 shows the growth of FutuBull, a low-cost integrated trading platform relative to another low-cost trading platform I-Access, which does not provide any financial information or corporate news on the trading platform.

The pattern shows that the growth of the integrated trading platform FutuBull has outpaced that of I-Access ever since FutuBull incorporated more information services on its trading platform in 2015.

Figure E-4 shows that the number of daily active users (i.e., the number of user accounts and visitors who access the platform at least once on a given trading day) has increased rapidly over time.

### APPENDIX F: REVISION NOTES ON REGISTERED PROPOSAL IN THE EXECUTION STAGE

Table 7

#1

In the execution stage of this registered report, it is observed that the main independent variables Sum Scaled Views (Results Announcements), Sum Scaled Views (Summary Reports), and Sum Scaled Views (Expert Opinions) have a right-skewed distribution with many large values. This is due to their small denominators having close to zero economic interest held by FutuBull clients. Winsorizing the data using a 1% cutoff does not quite address the issue, as its prevalence is more than 1%. To address this small denominator problem, table 7 introduces an additional sample selection condition (that is not mentioned in the registered proposal) by requiring firms to have over 10,000 HKD economic interest held by FutuBull clients to prevent small denominators from blowing up these Sum Scaled Views measures.

##### #2

In the registered proposal, table 7 consists of both panels A and B, where panel B conducts subsampling analyses based on the level of institutional ownership. While both analyses have been conducted in the execution stage, the main specification in panel A does not find any significant associations in the main independent variables. Similarly, there are no significant coefficients or differences in the main independent variables in the subsampling analyses in panel B. To trim down on report length, the registered report no longer presents the results of the tests in panel B as the results do not contribute much to further our understanding of the topic.

##### VARIABLE MEASUREMENT

Views: The registered proposal subtracts the minimum view counts on the “monthly return of equity issuer on movement in securities” within the quarter from the raw view counts as the Views measure in tables 3 and 4 in order to mitigate the concerns that some views are generated by internal vetting or other systematic procedures. As the distribution is highly right-skewed, the registered proposal also takes the logarithmic transformations on view counts when used in regressions. In the execution stage, the adjustment may result in negative numbers before log transformation if the view counts on some posts are smaller than the benchmarks due to either low view counts on some uninformative posts or abnormally high view counts on the benchmarking filings (especially when bundled with other contents). When the original adjustment results in negative numbers before log transformation, the registered report will use the median view counts on the benchmarking filing across the entire sample period for each firm as the adjustment factor. If it still results in negative numbers, the registered report will use the global minimum view counts on all posts (i.e., 200) as the adjustment factor.

Title Sentiment: In the registered proposal, the Sentiment variable is used to represent the sentiment in article titles. It is measured using the extent of positive sentiment in the press article title based on the BERT model with supervised fine-tuning trained using hand-labeled data. In execution, the BERT model is fine-tuned with multiclass classification with three categories: positive, neutral, and negative. As the negative sentiment part is

likely also informative, the title sentiment measure has been refined to incorporate the information from negative sentiment prediction. In the registered report, two variables are being used to represent the title sentiment of articles: an indicator Positive that equals one if the predicted probability of positive sentiment is higher than the predicted probability of negative sentiment by 25% as well as an indicator Negative that equals one if the predicted probability of negative sentiment is higher than the predicted probability of positive sentiment by 25% based on the BERT model. These two indicators are used to represent title sentiments in regressions in place of Sentiment.

Holding Changes: The registered report uses percentage differences to calculate holding changes instead of percentage changes mentioned in the proposal to mitigate the zero-denominator problem when the initial holding is 0.

##### OTHER MINOR CHANGES

Table 8, panel A: In the comparison between brokers, the omitted category has been changed from benchmarking brokers I-Access in columns (1) and (2) and Bulge Bracket in columns (3) and (4) to FutuBull in all the columns. This is to facilitate easier interpretation of the coefficient on News so that it can directly inform readers how FutuBull clients are trading with respect to the news in disclosures in various windows in this accounting disclosure sample. Therefore, the indicator variable in the interaction term has been changed from Futu to I-Access in columns (1) and (2) as well as to Bulge in columns (3) and (4).

Table 4, panel A, and table 10: While media articles are abundant overall, it turns out that media coverage is highly concentrated on only a subset of firms listed in HK. Most firms have no Summary Reports or Expert Opinion articles around earnings announcements. Therefore, the sample in table 4, panel A, is much smaller than expected when the specification requires the presence of multiple types of documents for each event. With a small sample, some variables with little variations cannot be estimated in the regression due to collinearity with other variables or firm-fixed effects. For example, there is almost no variation in the Topics or Highlight indicators in these samples as Summary Reports or Expert Opinion articles around earnings announcements are generally not related to a particular investment theme or promoted as headline news on the platform. In these two tables with a relatively small sample, the report uses # in the table to represent planned variables that are automatically dropped out in the estimation process due to the lack of variations or collinearity with other variables/fixed effects.

Table 9: Analyses on PEAD opportunities have been deemphasized and moved to the additional analyses section with a trimmed-down discussion. This is partly due to the results in earlier parts suggesting that self-directed retail investors may use a heterogeneous set of trading strategies, with aggregate trading directions on average not in line with capturing PEAD opportunities. Therefore, the registered report relies more on the infor-

mativeness test (table 6) that does not rely on using a particular trading strategy to make inferences, instead of focusing too much on interpreting trading directions only.

The untabulated additional analyses mentioned in section 4.3.2 are currently discussed in footnote 29 instead of the additional analyses section.

Variable Name: The Profit Updates variable has been renamed as Results Guidance with no change in the underlying definition.
